AI365 3.0 – M Cosmetic Email Agent Knowledge Base
============================================================
Source: ai365-Mcosmetic(1).html
Snapshot date: 2026-10-22
1. SYSTEM OVERVIEW
--------------------
This knowledge base represents the embedded snapshot used by the standalone Ai365 3.0 M Cosmetic Email Agent screen.
The system processes incoming customer emails, classifies the business intent, extracts structured entities, groups emails into work Cases, performs ERP-related work, creates reply drafts, and tracks processing status.
The main work categories are:
- quote: 견적요청
- order: 주문등록
- ship: 출하일정
- stock: 재고문의
- invoice: 인보이스 발행요청
- etc: 기타

Main navigation:
- Dashboard
- 수신메일
- 견적요청
- 주문등록
- 출하일정
- 재고문의
- 인보이스 발행요청
- 기타
- 업무 Case
- 처리오류

2. EMAIL PROCESSING RULES AND WORKFLOW
-----------------------------------
Incoming email workflow:
1) Receive customer email.
2) Classify the email into a business category.
3) Detect the intent.
4) Extract entities such as customer, item, quantity, delivery date, customer code, item code, currency, shipping address, quote reference and Sales Order.
5) Assign or create a work Case.
6) Determine whether ERP lookup, reply generation, order registration, shipping lookup, inventory lookup or invoice processing is required.
7) Execute the required ERP/API work.
8) Generate a reply draft when applicable.
9) Send the email after confirmation, or place it on hold/review when required.

AI confidence tiers:
- 95% 이상: 자동분류 — 108 items
- 80 ~ 94%: 자동분류, 확인 권장 — 19 items
- 80% 미만: 수신메일에서 담당자 확인 — 6 items

Inbox review rule:
- Emails that AI cannot classify or that lack required values are reviewed in the Inbox.
- After review, the email moves to the appropriate business menu.

3. REQUIRED FIELDS BY BUSINESS CATEGORY
--------------------------------------
- quote: customerCode, itemCode, quantity
- order: customerCode, itemCode, quantity, deliveryDate, shippingAddress
- ship: customerCode
- stock: customerCode, itemCode
- invoice: customerCode, salesOrder
- etc: No required fields

4. ERP PROCESSING DEFINITIONS
------------------------------
[quote]
- act: ERP 단가 조회
- done: 단가조회 완료
- ws: 견적작성 필요
- wsDone: 단가조회 완료
- mail: 견적 답변 이메일
- hint: ERP에서 단가·재고·납기를 조회한 뒤 AI가 답변 초안을 작성합니다
- api: GET /api/erp/v1/prices

[ship]
- act: ERP 출하내역 조회
- done: 출하내역 조회 완료
- ws: ERP 조회필요
- wsDone: 조회완료
- mail: 출하일정 안내 이메일
- hint: ERP에서 출하내역을 조회한 뒤 AI가 안내 메일을 작성합니다
- api: GET /api/erp/v1/shipments

[stock]
- act: ERP 재고 조회
- done: 재고조회 완료
- ws: 재고 조회필요
- wsDone: 재고조회 완료
- mail: 재고 안내 이메일
- hint: ERP에서 On-hand 재고를 조회한 뒤 AI가 답변 메일을 작성합니다
- api: GET /api/erp/v1/inventory/on-hand

[invoice]
- act: ERP Sales Order 조회
- done: Sales Order 조회 완료
- ws: 주문정보 조회필요
- wsDone: 주문정보 조회완료
- mail: 인보이스 발송 이메일
- hint: ERP에서 Sales Order 정보를 조회해 인보이스를 만들고 발송 메일을 작성합니다
- api: GET /api/erp/v1/sales-orders
